Scraper Endpoint lacking InfoHash attribute (torentio.strem.fun)\",\"articleBody\":\"## Prerequisites\\n- [x] I have checked the [existing issues](https://github.com/rivenmedia/riven/issues) and confirmed this is not a duplicate  \\n- [x] I am running the latest version of Riven  \\n- [x] I have read the [documentation](https://github.com/rivenmedia/riven/blob/main/README.md)  \\n- [x] I have checked in [Discord channel](https://discord.com/invite/rivenmedia) for a solution  \\n\\n## Environment\\n- **Riven Version**: 0.23.6  \\n- **Deployment Method**: Docker (DMB stack)  \\n- **Operating System**: Linux (container host)  \\n\\n## Current Behavior\\nWhen scraping via the Torrentio scraper, Riven throws an exception:\\n\\n```text\\nTorrentio exception thrown: 'types.SimpleNamespace' object has no attribute 'infoHash'\\n```\\n\\nNo releases are returned from Torrentio for affected items, even though the underlying Torrentio endpoint is responding with valid Real-Debrid streams.\\n\\nThe traceback points at `torrentio.py` trying to access `stream.infoHash` on a `SimpleNamespace`, which does not have that attribute.\\n\\n## Expected Behavior\\n\\n- Riven should handle Torrentio’s current Real-Debrid stream format (URL-only, `url` + `behaviorHints`) without crashing.\\n- Streams that do not expose an `infoHash`/`info_hash` field should either:\\n    - Have the info hash derived from the RD resolve URL, or\\n    - Be skipped cleanly, without raising an exception.\\n\\nExisting setups where Torrentio still returns `infoHash` in the JSON should continue to work unchanged.\\n\\n## Steps To Reproduce\\n\\n1. \\\"staleRevalidate\\\": 14400,\\n  \\\"staleError\\\": 604800\\n}\\n```\\n\\nThere is **no** `infoHash`/`info_hash` field in the stream objects. The 40-character hash appears only inside the `url` path (for example: `59e11cef8c2152ac73681092844ebd3db19025bc`).\\n\\n### Pydantic model vs runtime object\\n\\nIn `src/program/services/scrapers/torrentio.py` the Pydantic model is:\\n\\n```python\\nclass TorrentioScrapeResponse(BaseModel):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Model for Torrentio scrape response\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    class Stream(BaseModel):\\n        title: str\\n        info_hash: str = Field(alias=\\\"infoHash\\\")\\n\\n    streams: list[Stream]\\n```\\n\\nSo when parsing works, `stream.info_hash` should be available (aliasing JSON `infoHash`). However, the traceback shows Riven accessing `stream.infoHash` on a `SimpleNamespace`, which suggests a code path is not using the Pydantic model and is assuming the old `infoHash` field always exists.\\n\\n### Suggested fix (backwards compatible)\\n\\nConceptually, the scraper could:\\n\\n- Treat `info_hash` as optional.\\n- If it is missing, derive a hash from `stream.url` (for RD-resolve URLs) by extracting the 40-char hex segment.\\n- Only skip streams where neither `info_hash` nor a hash segment can be found.\\n\\nExample pattern inside the `scrape` loop:\\n\\n```python\\ntorrents: dict[str, str] = {}\\n\\nfor stream in data.streams:\\n    # Prefer the Pydantic field first (old format with infoHash in JSON)\\n    info_hash = getattr(stream, \\\"info_hash\\\", None)\\n    url = getattr(stream, \\\"url\\\", None)\\n\\n    # Fallback: derive hash from RD resolve URL if no info_hash field\\n    if not info_hash and url:\\n        # Example RD URL:\\n        # https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/\\u003capi_key\\u003e/\\u003chash\\u003e/null/...\\n        parts = url.split(\\\"/\\\")\\n        for segment in parts:\\n            if len(segment) == 40 and all(c in \\\"0123456789abcdef\\\" for c in segment.lower()):\\n                info_hash = segment\\n                break\\n\\n    # If we still have no info_hash, skip this stream\\n    if not info_hash:\\n        continue\\n\\n    stream_title = (getattr(stream, \\\"title\\\", \\\"\\\") or \\\"\\\").split(\\\"\\\\n👤\\\")\\n    raw_title = stream_title.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    torrents[info_hash] = raw_title\\n```\\n\\nThis should:\\n\\n- Keep old Torrentio responses (with `infoHash` in JSON) working via `info_hash`.\\n- Support the new RD URL-only format by extracting the hash from `url`.\\n- Avoid crashes when a stream truly lacks any hash information.\",\"author\":{\"url\":\"https://github.com/zathrath03\",\"@type\":\"Person\",\"name\":\"zathrath03\"},\"datePublished\":\"2026-01-10T21:49:00.000Z\",\"interactionStatistic\":{\"@type\":\"InteractionCounter\",\"interactionType\":\"https://schema.org/CommentAction\",\"userInteractionCount\":3},\"url\":\"https://github.com/1342/riven/issues/1342\"}</script> **Operating System**: Linux (container host)  \\n\\n## Current Behavior\\nWhen scraping via the Torrentio scraper, Riven throws an exception:\\n\\n```text\\nTorrentio exception thrown: current filter) returns streams like:\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"streams\\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\\"name\\\": \\\"[RD+] Torrentio\\\\n1080p\\\",\\n      \\\"title\\\": \\\"Sketch 2024 1080p WEBRip\\\\n👤 100 💾 1.55 GB ⚙️ YTS\\\",\\n      \\\"url\\\": \\\"https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/REDACTED/59e11cef8c2152ac73681092844ebd3db19025bc/null/0/Sketch.2024.1080p.WEBRip.x265.10bit.AAC5.1-[YTS.MX].mp4\\\",\\n      \\\"behaviorHints\\\": {\\n        \\\"bingeGroup\\\": \\\"torrentio|1080p|WEBRip|x265|10bit\\\",\\n        \\\"filename\\\": \\\"Sketch.2024.1080p.WEBRip.x265.10bit.AAC5.1-[YTS.MX].mp4\\\"\\n      }\\n    },\\n    {\\n      \\\"name\\\": \\\"[RD download] Torrentio\\\\n720p\\\",\\n      \\\"title\\\": \\\"Sketch.2024.Multi.720P.-WEBRIP.mkv\\\\n👤 1 💾 2.34 GB ⚙️ Torrent9\\\\nMulti Audio / 🇫🇷\\\",\\n      \\\"url\\\": \\\"https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/READACTED/f220c0b73febb82151253f6c0d6cdc59e2851e8c/null/0/Sketch.2024.Multi.720P.-WEBRIP.mkv\\\",\\n      \\\"behaviorHints\\\": {\\n        \\\"bingeGroup\\\": \\\"torrentio|720p|WEBRip\\\",\\n        \\\"filename\\\": \\\"Sketch.2024.Multi.720P.-WEBRIP.mkv\\\"\\n      }\\n    }\\n  ],\\n  \\\"cacheMaxAge\\\": 3600,\\n  \\\"staleRevalidate\\\": 14400,\\n  \\\"staleError\\\": 604800\\n}\\n```\\n\\nThere is **no** `infoHash`/`info_hash` field in the stream objects. The 40-character hash appears only inside the `url` path (for example: `59e11cef8c2152ac73681092844ebd3db19025bc`).\\n\\n### Pydantic model vs runtime object\\n\\nIn `src/program/services/scrapers/torrentio.py` the Pydantic model is:\\n\\n```python\\nclass TorrentioScrapeResponse(BaseModel):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Model for Torrentio scrape response\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    class Stream(BaseModel):\\n        title: str\\n        info_hash: str = Field(alias=\\\"infoHash\\\")\\n\\n    streams: list[Stream]\\n```\\n\\nSo when parsing works, `stream.info_hash` should be available (aliasing JSON `infoHash`). However, the traceback shows Riven accessing `stream.infoHash` on a `SimpleNamespace`, which suggests a code path is not using the Pydantic model and is assuming the old `infoHash` field always exists.\\n\\n### Suggested fix (backwards compatible)\\n\\nConceptually, the scraper could:\\n\\n- Treat `info_hash` as optional.\\n- If it is missing, derive a hash from `stream.url` (for RD-resolve URLs) by extracting the 40-char hex segment.\\n- Only skip streams where neither `info_hash` nor a hash segment can be found.\\n\\nExample pattern inside the `scrape` loop:\\n\\n```python\\ntorrents: dict[str, str] = {}\\n\\nfor stream in data.streams:\\n    # Prefer the Pydantic field first (old format with infoHash in JSON)\\n    info_hash = getattr(stream, \\\"info_hash\\\", None)\\n    url = getattr(stream, \\\"url\\\", None)\\n\\n    # Fallback: derive hash from RD resolve URL if no info_hash field\\n    if not info_hash and url:\\n        # Example RD URL:\\n        # https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/\\u003capi_key\\u003e/\\u003chash\\u003e/null/...\\n        parts = url.split(\\\"/\\\")\\n        for segment in parts:\\n            if len(segment) == 40 and all(c in \\\"0123456789abcdef\\\" for c in segment.lower()):\\n                info_hash = segment\\n                break\\n\\n    # If we still have no info_hash, skip this stream\\n    if not info_hash:\\n        continue\\n\\n    stream_title = (getattr(stream, \\\"title\\\", \\\"\\\") or \\\"\\\").split(\\\"\\\\n👤\\\")\\n    raw_title = stream_title.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    torrents[info_hash] = raw_title\\n```\\n\\nThis should:\\n\\n- Keep old Torrentio responses (with `infoHash` in JSON) working via `info_hash`.\\n- Support the new RD URL-only format by extracting the hash from `url`.\\n- Avoid crashes when a stream truly lacks any hash information.\",\"author\":{\"url\":\"https://github.com/zathrath03\",\"@type\":\"Person\",\"name\":\"zathrath03\"},\"datePublished\":\"2026-01-10T21:49:00.000Z\",\"interactionStatistic\":{\"@type\":\"InteractionCounter\",\"interactionType\":\"https://schema.org/CommentAction\",\"userInteractionCount\":3},\"url\":\"https://github.com/1342/riven/issues/1342\"},\"best_match_sort_enabled\":false,\"issue_search_type\":\"ISSUE_HYBRID\",\"preloadedQueries\":[{\"queryId\":\"1f80edbc76615a49dc57fe5fa9a17002\",\"queryName\":\"IssueViewerViewQuery\",\"variables\":{\"count\":15,\"number\":1342,\"owner\":\"rivenmedia\",\"repo\":\"riven\"},\"result\":{\"data\":{\"repository\":{\"id\":\"R_kgDOK3OoEw\",\"isOwnerEnterpriseManaged\":false,\"owner\":{\"__typename\":\"Organization\",\"userNameDisplayConfiguration\":\"HANDLE\",\"id\":\"O_kgDOCkOdvA\"},\"issue\":{\"id\":\"I_kwDOK3OoE87ihaDs\",\"number\":1342,\"viewerCanUpdateMetadata\":false,\"repository\":{\"name\":\"riven\",\"isArchived\":false,\"owner\":{\"__typename\":\"Organization\",\"login\":\"rivenmedia\",\"id\":\"O_kgDOCkOdvA\",\"url\":\"https://github.com/rivenmedia\",\"databaseId\":172203452,\"issueFieldsEnabled\":true,\"issueFields\":{\"totalCount\":0},\"pinnedIssueFields\":{\"nodes\":[]}},\"id\":\"R_kgDOK3OoEw\",\"nameWithOwner\":\"rivenmedia/riven\",\"isWritable\":true,\"isPrivate\":false,\"databaseId\":728999955,\"slashCommandsEnabled\":false,\"visibility\":\"PUBLIC\",\"viewerCanPush\":false,\"viewerCanInteract\":false,\"viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML\":\"\",\"isCopilotAgentEnabled\":null,\"pinnedIssues\":{\"totalCount\":1},\"viewerCanPinIssues\":false,\"viewerIssueCreationPermissions\":{\"triageable\":false},\"issueTypes\":{\"edges\":[{\"node\":{\"id\":\"IT_kwDOCkOdvM4BWEH0\"}},{\"node\":{\"id\":\"IT_kwDOCkOdvM4BWEH1\"}},{\"node\":{\"id\":\"IT_kwDOCkOdvM4BWEH2\"}}]},\"isOwnerEnterpriseManaged\":false},\"updatedAt\":\"2026-03-26T04:46:51Z\",\"title\":\"[Issue] current filter) returns streams like:\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"streams\\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\\"name\\\": \\\"[RD+] Torrentio\\\\n1080p\\\",\\n      \\\"title\\\": \\\"Sketch 2024 1080p WEBRip\\\\n👤 100 💾 1.55 GB ⚙️ YTS\\\",\\n      \\\"url\\\": \\\"https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/REDACTED/59e11cef8c2152ac73681092844ebd3db19025bc/null/0/Sketch.2024.1080p.WEBRip.x265.10bit.AAC5.1-[YTS.MX].mp4\\\",\\n      \\\"behaviorHints\\\": {\\n        \\\"bingeGroup\\\": \\\"torrentio|1080p|WEBRip|x265|10bit\\\",\\n        \\\"filename\\\": \\\"Sketch.2024.1080p.WEBRip.x265.10bit.AAC5.1-[YTS.MX].mp4\\\"\\n      }\\n    },\\n    {\\n      \\\"name\\\": \\\"[RD download] Torrentio\\\\n720p\\\",\\n      \\\"title\\\": \\\"Sketch.2024.Multi.720P.-WEBRIP.mkv\\\\n👤 1 💾 2.34 GB ⚙️ Torrent9\\\\nMulti Audio / 🇫🇷\\\",\\n      \\\"url\\\": \\\"https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/READACTED/f220c0b73febb82151253f6c0d6cdc59e2851e8c/null/0/Sketch.2024.Multi.720P.-WEBRIP.mkv\\\",\\n      \\\"behaviorHints\\\": {\\n        \\\"bingeGroup\\\": \\\"torrentio|720p|WEBRip\\\",\\n        \\\"filename\\\": \\\"Sketch.2024.Multi.720P.-WEBRIP.mkv\\\"\\n      }\\n    }\\n  ],\\n  \\\"cacheMaxAge\\\": 3600,\\n  \\\"staleRevalidate\\\": 14400,\\n  \\\"staleError\\\": 604800\\n}\\n```\\n\\nThere is **no** `infoHash`/`info_hash` field in the stream objects. The 40-character hash appears only inside the `url` path (for example: `59e11cef8c2152ac73681092844ebd3db19025bc`).\\n\\n### Pydantic model vs runtime object\\n\\nIn `src/program/services/scrapers/torrentio.py` the Pydantic model is:\\n\\n```python\\nclass TorrentioScrapeResponse(BaseModel):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Model for Torrentio scrape response\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    class Stream(BaseModel):\\n        title: str\\n        info_hash: str = Field(alias=\\\"infoHash\\\")\\n\\n    streams: list[Stream]\\n```\\n\\nSo when parsing works, `stream.info_hash` should be available (aliasing JSON `infoHash`). However, the traceback shows Riven accessing `stream.infoHash` on a `SimpleNamespace`, which suggests a code path is not using the Pydantic model and is assuming the old `infoHash` field always exists.\\n\\n### Suggested fix (backwards compatible)\\n\\nConceptually, the scraper could:\\n\\n- Treat `info_hash` as optional.\\n- If it is missing, derive a hash from `stream.url` (for RD-resolve URLs) by extracting the 40-char hex segment.\\n- Only skip streams where neither `info_hash` nor a hash segment can be found.\\n\\nExample pattern inside the `scrape` loop:\\n\\n```python\\ntorrents: dict[str, str] = {}\\n\\nfor stream in data.streams:\\n    # Prefer the Pydantic field first (old format with infoHash in JSON)\\n    info_hash = getattr(stream, \\\"info_hash\\\", None)\\n    url = getattr(stream, \\\"url\\\", None)\\n\\n    # Fallback: derive hash from RD resolve URL if no info_hash field\\n    if not info_hash and url:\\n        # Example RD URL:\\n        # https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/\\u003capi_key\\u003e/\\u003chash\\u003e/null/...\\n        parts = url.split(\\\"/\\\")\\n        for segment in parts:\\n            if len(segment) == 40 and all(c in \\\"0123456789abcdef\\\" for c in segment.lower()):\\n                info_hash = segment\\n                break\\n\\n    # If we still have no info_hash, skip this stream\\n    if not info_hash:\\n        continue\\n\\n    stream_title = (getattr(stream, \\\"title\\\", \\\"\\\") or \\\"\\\").split(\\\"\\\\n👤\\\")\\n    raw_title = stream_title.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    torrents[info_hash] = raw_title\\n```\\n\\nThis should:\\n\\n- Keep old Torrentio responses (with `infoHash` in JSON) working via `info_hash`.\\n- Support the new RD URL-only format by extracting the hash from `url`.\\n- Avoid crashes when a stream truly lacks any hash information.\",\"bodyHTML\":\"\\u003ch2 dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003ePrerequisites\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\n\\u003cul Method</strong>: Docker (DMB stack)</li>\n<li><strong>Operating System</strong>: Linux (container host)</li>\n</ul>\n<h2 dir=\"auto\">Current Behavior</h2>\n<p dir=\"auto\">When scraping via the Torrentio scraper, Riven throws an exception:</p>\n<div class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\"><pre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"><code class=\"notranslate\">Torrentio exception thrown: 'types.SimpleNamespace' object has no attribute 'infoHash'\n</code></pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container position-absolute right-0 top-0\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0\" data-copy-feedback=\"Copied!\" data-tooltip-direction=\"w\" value=\"Torrentio exception 1.5h-1.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v7.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h7.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-1.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0v1.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 9.25 16h-7.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 14.25Z\"></path><path d=\"M5 1.75C5 .784 5.784 0 1 1.042-.018L6 10.94l6.72-6.72a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0Z\"></path>\n</svg>\n    </clipboard-copy>\n  </div></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">No releases are returned from Torrentio for affected items, even though the underlying Torrentio endpoint is responding with valid Real-Debrid streams.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The traceback points at <code class=\"notranslate\">torrentio.py</code> trying to access <code class=\"notranslate\">stream.infoHash</code> on a <code class=\"notranslate\">SimpleNamespace</code>, which does not have that attribute.</p>\n<h2 dir=\"auto\">Expected Behavior</h2>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Riven should handle Torrentio’s current Real-Debrid stream format (URL-only, <code class=\"notranslate\">url</code> + <code class=\"notranslate\">behaviorHints</code>) without crashing.</li>\n<li>Streams that do not expose an <code class=\"notranslate\">infoHash</code>/<code class=\"notranslate\">info_hash</code> field should either:\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Have the info hash derived from the RD resolve URL, or</li>\n<li>Be skipped cleanly, without raising an exception.</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Existing setups where Torrentio still returns <code class=\"notranslate\">infoHash</code> in the JSON should continue to work unchanged.</p>\n<h2 dir=\"auto\">Steps To Reproduce</h2>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Configure the Torrentio scraper in <code class=\"notranslate\">config.json</code> as follows:</li>\n</ol>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\"><pre class=\"notranslate\">infoHash</code>/<code class=\"notranslate\">info_hash</code> field in the stream objects. The 40-character hash appears only inside the <code class=\"notranslate\">url</code> path (for example: <code class=\"notranslate\">59e11cef8c2152ac73681092844ebd3db19025bc</code>).</p>\n<h3 dir=\"auto\">Pydantic model vs runtime object</h3>\n<p dir=\"auto\">In <code class=\"notranslate\">src/program/services/scrapers/torrentio.py</code> the Pydantic model is:</p>\n<div class=\"highlight class=\"zeroclipboard-container position-absolute right-0 top-0\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0\" parsing works, <code class=\"notranslate\">stream.info_hash</code> should be available (aliasing JSON <code class=\"notranslate\">infoHash</code>). However, the traceback shows Riven accessing <code class=\"notranslate\">stream.infoHash</code> on a <code class=\"notranslate\">SimpleNamespace</code>, which suggests a code path is not using the Pydantic model and is assuming the old <code class=\"notranslate\">infoHash</code> field always exists.</p>\n<h3 dir=\"auto\">Suggested fix (backwards compatible)</h3>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Conceptually, the scraper could:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Treat <code class=\"notranslate\">info_hash</code> as optional.</li>\n<li>If it is missing, derive a hash from <code class=\"notranslate\">stream.url</code> (for RD-resolve URLs) by extracting the 40-char hex segment.</li>\n<li>Only skip streams where neither <code class=\"notranslate\">info_hash</code> nor a hash segment can be found.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Example pattern inside the <code class=\"notranslate\">scrape</code> loop:</p>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\"><pre class=\"notranslate\"><span class=\"pl-s1\">torrents</span>: <span class=\"pl-s1\">dict</span>[<span class=\"pl-smi\">str</span>, <span class=\"pl-smi\">str</span>] <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> {}\n\n<span class=\"pl-k\">for</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">stream</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">in</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">data</span>.<span class=\"pl-c1\">streams</span>:\n    <span class=\"pl-c\"># Prefer the Pydantic field first (old format with infoHash in JSON)</span>\n    <span class=\"pl-s1\">info_hash</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-en\">getattr</span>(<span class=\"pl-s1\">stream</span>, <span class=\"pl-s\">\"info_hash\"</span>, <span class=\"pl-c1\">None</span>)\n    <span class=\"pl-s1\">url</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-en\">getattr</span>(<span class=\"pl-s1\">stream</span>, <span class=\"pl-s\">\"url\"</span>, <span class=\"pl-c1\">None</span>)\n\n    <span class=\"pl-c\"># Fallback: derive hash from RD resolve URL if no info_hash field</span>\n    <span class=\"pl-k\">if</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">not</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">info_hash</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">and</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">url</span>:\n        <span class=\"pl-c\"># Example RD URL:</span>\n        <span class=\"pl-c\"># https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/&lt;api_key&gt;/&lt;hash&gt;/null/...</span>\n        <span class=\"pl-s1\">parts</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">url</span>.<span class=\"pl-c1\">split</span>(<span class=\"pl-s\">\"/\"</span>)\n        <span class=\"pl-k\">for</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">segment</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">in</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">parts</span>:\n            <span class=\"pl-k\">if</span> <span class=\"pl-en\">len</span>(<span class=\"pl-s1\">segment</span>) <span class=\"pl-c1\">==</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">40</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">and</span> <span class=\"pl-en\">all</span>(<span class=\"pl-s1\">c</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">in</span> <span class=\"pl-s\">\"0123456789abcdef\"</span> <span class=\"pl-k\">for</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">c</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">in</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">segment</span>.<span class=\"pl-c1\">lower</span>()):\n                <span class=\"pl-s1\">info_hash</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">segment</span>\n                <span class=\"pl-k\">break</span>\n\n    <span class=\"pl-c\"># If we still have no info_hash, skip this stream</span>\n    <span class=\"pl-k\">if</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">not</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">info_hash</span>:\n        <span class=\"pl-k\">continue</span>\n\n    <span class=\"pl-s1\">stream_title</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> (<span class=\"pl-en\">getattr</span>(<span class=\"pl-s1\">stream</span>, <span class=\"pl-s\">\"title\"</span>, <span class=\"pl-s\">\"\"</span>) <span class=\"pl-c1\">or</span> <span class=\"pl-s\">\"\"</span>).<span class=\"pl-c1\">split</span>(<span class=\"pl-s\">\"<span class=\"pl-cce\">\\n</span>👤\"</span>)\n    <span class=\"pl-s1\">raw_title</span> <span class=\"pl-c1\">=</span> <span class=\"pl-s1\">stream_title</span>.<span class=\"pl-c1\">split</span>(<span class=\"pl-s\">\"<span class=\"pl-cce\">\\n</span>\"</span>)\n    <span position-absolute right-0 top-0\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0\" data-copy-feedback=\"Copied!\" data-tooltip-direction=\"w\" value=\"torrents: dict[str, str] = {}\n\nfor stream in data.streams:\n    # Prefer the Pydantic field first (old format with infoHash in JSON)\n    info_hash = getattr(stream, &quot;info_hash&quot;, None)\n    url = getattr(stream, &quot;url&quot;, None)\n\n    # Fallback: derive hash from RD resolve URL if no info_hash field\n    if not info_hash and url:\n        # Example RD URL:\n        # https://torrentio.strem.fun/resolve/realdebrid/&lt;api_key&gt;/&lt;hash&gt;/null/...\n        parts = url.split(&quot;/&quot;)\n        for segment in parts:\n (with <code class=\"notranslate\">infoHash</code> in JSON) working via <code class=\"notranslate\">info_hash</code>.</li>\n<li>Support the new RD URL-only format by extracting the hash from <code class=\"notranslate\">url</code>.</li>\n<li>Avoid crashes when a stream truly lacks any hash information.</li>\n</ul></div></div><div class=\"IssueBodyViewer-module__IssueBodyTaskList__a_Inr\"><div
